# A56 — Library, queues, and reading records: iOS guarantees against Android

Read-only static audit, 2026-10-15. **Android keeps most of the tested reader behavior, including note salvage, exact-passage highlight matching, reader-position transport, background session checkpoints, the statistics mathematics, and the checked Hide-mode exclusions.** The main gaps are destructive actions acting on restored records, deletion sweeps outside the persistence gate, incomplete revival/deduplication on reacquisition, and queue/collection lifecycle bookkeeping.

Android evidence is exclusively **`0498ef2a0f698e7683feada50c8861690aaefb1b`**, read using `git show` / `git grep` in `/Users/cidy02/kudos-android-lane`. Its checked-out HEAD was already `0ffa19ddc824e0ef1caa78ca071759e6b79f1f5a`; those newer files were not substituted. iOS evidence is `/Users/cidy02/kudos-ios-polish`, HEAD **`76473fc9571798070c0d11ed7cbdc157ead222a9`**, with no tracked edits. Existing untracked iOS Packages/Vendor and Android digest-input were left alone. `KudosTests/…` and `kudos-ao3-reader/…` below resolve in that iOS worktree; `android/…` resolves in the pinned Android tree.

Binding: [DATA_AND_PERSISTENCE_INVARIANTS.md](../../DATA_AND_PERSISTENCE_INVARIANTS.md). Followed A52/A54/A55’s method: compare the reader guarantee with implementation and connected callers, not test names. **Kept is a source judgment, not an executed test result.** Missing requires a quoted divergent path; an absent Android test is not a defect. Not applicable identifies an absent platform feature/API, including dormant helper features. Unsure is an unproved comparison even when neighboring code is quoted. Representation differences (timestamps versus a Swift pending-status enum, byte input versus a temporary-file URL) are called out. Literal state differences that affect preservation/queue bookkeeping are not hidden behind a successful file check.

**Coverage: all 225 `@Test` functions in all 24 requested files, with all 648 `#expect` sites quoted: 184 kept, 20 missing, 20 not applicable, 1 unsure.** Parameterized tests and assertions inside loops are one source test/site each. Several missing rows share a root cause; they are not 20 independent bugs. Quotes collapse source whitespace for table layout; line ranges are inclusive. Android tests named in the final column identify related coverage in the pinned sources, with narrower coverage identified; none was run. “No specific test located” is a search boundary, not proof that no relevant test exists.

## LibrarySortTests

History/Favorites are right, but the other five default-display cases are not kept.<br>Android test: no specific matching test located. |
| `everyWorkSectionNamesItsOrder`<br>Each work section names its actual order. | `KudosTests/LibrarySectionKindTests.swift:97` <code>#expect(!kind.orderDescription.isEmpty, "\(kind) has no order phrase")</code><br>`KudosTests/LibrarySectionKindTests.swift:99` <code>#expect(LibrarySectionKind.readingNow.orderDescription == "most recently read first")</code><br>`KudosTests/LibrarySectionKindTests.swift:100` <code>#expect(LibrarySectionKind.savedForLater.orderDescription == "most recently read or added first")</code><br>`KudosTests/LibrarySectionKindTests.swift:101` <code>#expect(LibrarySectionKind.downloaded.orderDescription == "newest first")</code><br>`KudosTests/LibrarySectionKindTests.swift:102` <code>#expect(LibrarySectionKind.collections.orderDescription.isEmpty)</code> | **kept** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/library/LibraryScreen.kt:1425–1441` <code>} else if (kind == LibrarySectionKind.History) { LibraryHistoryGrouping.tallyLine(sectionItems.size, state.historyGrouping, groupedItems) } else { val sortLabel = if (state.hasActiveQueryOrFilters) null else { if (state.sort != LibrarySort.Natural) state.sort.label else when (kind) { // iOS `LibrarySectionKind.orderDescription`: each says what // `LibraryQuery` sorts that section by. Finished is by last // read, not by when it was finished; Saved for Later by last // read, else added; the other two by date added. ## LibraryHistoryGroupingTests

| Test — reader guarantee | iOS assertion quote | Verdict | Kotlin implementation quote | Consequence / Android test |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `timeBucketsKeepTheirSourceOrderRatherThanSortingBySize`<br>Time buckets keep chronological section order even when Earlier is larger. | `KudosTests/LibraryHistoryGroupingTests.swift:57` <code>#expect(titles(.time, [today, old1, old2, old3]) == ["Today", "Earlier"])</code> | **kept** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/library/LibraryHistoryGrouping.kt:98–102` <code>): List&lt;Bucket&gt; = when (grouping) { Flat -&gt; if (works.isEmpty()) emptyList() else listOf(Bucket(FlatTitle, works.map { it.id })) Time -&gt; ordered(TimeBucket.entries.map { it.title }, works) { TimeBucket.bucket(it.lastReadDate, now, zone, weekFields).title }</code><br>`android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/library/LibraryHistoryGrouping.kt:159–168` <code>private fun ordered( order: List&lt;String&gt;, works: List&lt;SavedWork&gt;, key: (SavedWork) -&gt; String ): List&lt;Bucket&gt; { val buckets = HashMap&lt;String, MutableList&lt;String&gt;&gt;() for (work in works) buckets.getOrPut(key(work)) { mutableListOf() }.add(work.id) return order.mapNotNull { title -&gt; buckets[title]?.takeIf { it.isNotEmpty() }?.let { Bucket(title, it.toList()) } }</code> | Android test: LibraryHistoryGroupingTests.timeBucketsKeepTheirSourceOrderRatherThanSortingBySize |
| `aWorkNeverOpenedIsItsOwnBucketNotTheOldestOne`<br>Never opened is distinct from Earlier. | `KudosTests/LibraryHistoryGroupingTests.swift:67` <code>#expect(buckets == ["Earlier", "Never opened"])</code> | **kept** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/library/LibraryHistoryGrouping.kt:33–39` <code>enum class TimeBucket(val title: String) { Today("Today"), Yesterday("Yesterday"), ThisWeek("This week"), ThisMonth("This month"), Earlier("Earlier"), Never("Never opened");</code><br>`android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/library/LibraryHistoryGrouping.kt:53–58` <code>if (date == null) return Never val day = date.atZone(zone).toLocalDate() val today = now.atZone(zone).toLocalDate() if (day == today) return Today // iOS subtracts one minute from today's midnight, including skipped civil days. if (day == today.atStartOfDay(zone).minusSeconds(60).toLocalDate()) return Yesterday</code> | Android test: LibraryHistoryGroupingTests.aWorkNeverOpenedIsItsOwnBucketNotTheOldestOne |
| `yesterdayIsMeasuredFromNowRatherThanTheWallClock`<br>Yesterday uses the supplied clock and calendar day. | `KudosTests/LibraryHistoryGroupingTests.swift:72` <code>#expect(titles(.time, [yesterday]) == ["Yesterday"])</code> | **kept** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/library/LibraryHistoryGrouping.kt:53–58` <code>if (date == null) return Never val day = date.atZone(zone).toLocalDate() val today = now.atZone(zone).toLocalDate() if (day == today) return Today // iOS subtracts one minute from today's midnight, including skipped civil days. if (day == today.atStartOfDay(zone).minusSeconds(60).toLocalDate()) return Yesterday</code> | Android test: LibraryHistoryGroupingTests.yesterdayIsMeasuredFromNowRatherThanTheWallClock |
| `emptyBucketsAreDroppedRatherThanDrawnEmpty`<br>Empty buckets are omitted. | `KudosTests/LibraryHistoryGroupingTests.swift:76` <code>#expect(titles(.time, [work("Today", lastRead: now)]) == ["Today"])</code> | **kept** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/library/LibraryHistoryGrouping.kt:159–168` <code>private fun ordered( order: List&lt;String&gt;, works: List&lt;SavedWork&gt;, key: (SavedWork) -&gt; String ): List&lt;Bucket&gt; { val buckets = HashMap&lt;String, MutableList&lt;String&gt;&gt;() for (work in works) buckets.getOrPut(key(work)) { mutableListOf() }.add(work.id) return order.mapNotNull { title -&gt; buckets[title]?.takeIf { it.isNotEmpty() }?.let { Bucket(title, it.toList()) } }</code> | Android test: LibraryHistoryGroupingTests.emptyBucketsAreDroppedRatherThanDrawnEmpty |
| `aFreedFileThatWasNeverFinishedIsReadNotFinished`<br>A freed, unfinished work is Read, not finished. | `KudosTests/LibraryHistoryGroupingTests.swift:85` <code>#expect(titles(.state, [freed]) == ["Read, not finished"])</code> | **kept** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/library/LibraryHistoryGrouping.kt:109–116` <code>) { work -&gt; when { work.isFinished -&gt; FinishedTitle !work.hasEpub -&gt; ReadNotFinishedTitle !work.hasStartedReading -&gt; NotStartedTitle isAbandoned(work) -&gt; AbandonedTitle else -&gt; InProgressTitle }</code> | Android test: LibraryHistoryGroupingTests.aFreedFileThatWasNeverFinishedIsReadNotFinished |
| `abandonedWinsOverInProgressSoNoWorkIsCountedTwice`<br>Abandoned takes precedence over In progress without double-counting. | `KudosTests/LibraryHistoryGroupingTests.swift:96` <code>#expect(buckets.map(\.title) == ["In progress", "Abandoned"])</code><br>`KudosTests/LibraryHistoryGroupingTests.swift:97` <code>#expect(buckets.flatMap(\.workIDs).count == 2)</code><br>`KudosTests/LibraryHistoryGroupingTests.swift:99` <code>#expect(Set(buckets.flatMap(\.workIDs)).count == 2)</code> | **kept** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/library/LibraryHistoryGrouping.kt:106–116` <code>State -&gt; ordered( listOf(InProgressTitle, AbandonedTitle, ReadNotFinishedTitle, FinishedTitle, NotStartedTitle), works ) { work -&gt; when { work.isFinished -&gt; FinishedTitle !work.hasEpub -&gt; ReadNotFinishedTitle !work.hasStartedReading -&gt; NotStartedTitle isAbandoned(work) -&gt; AbandonedTitle else -&gt; InProgressTitle }</code><br>`android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/library/LibraryHistoryGrouping.kt:164–168` <code>val buckets = HashMap&lt;String, MutableList&lt;String&gt;&gt;() for (work in works) buckets.getOrPut(key(work)) { mutableListOf() }.add(work.id) return order.mapNotNull { title -&gt; buckets[title]?.takeIf { it.isNotEmpty() }?.let { Bucket(title, it.toList()) } }</code> | Android test: LibraryHistoryGroupingTests.abandonedWinsOverInProgressSoNoWorkIsCountedTwice |
| `stateTallyCountsInProgressAndAbandonedAndDropsZeros`<br>State tallies count In progress and Abandoned and omit zero figures. | `KudosTests/LibraryHistoryGroupingTests.swift:113–114` <code>#expect(LibraryHistoryGrouping.tallyLine(workCount: 4, grouping: .state, buckets: buckets) == "4 works · 2 in progress · 1 abandoned")</code><br>`KudosTests/LibraryHistoryGroupingTests.swift:119–120` <code>#expect(LibraryHistoryGrouping.tallyLine(workCount: 2, grouping: .state, buckets: noneAbandoned) == "2 works · 1 in progress")</code> | **kept** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/library/LibraryHistoryGrouping.kt:141–154` <code>fun tallyLine(workCount: Int, grouping: LibraryHistoryGrouping, buckets: List&lt;Bucket&gt;): String { val works = "$workCount ${if (workCount == 1) "work" else "works"}" return when (grouping) { Time, Flat -&gt; "$works · most recently read first" Fandom -&gt; works State -&gt; { val inProgress = buckets.firstOrNull { it.title == InProgressTitle }?.workIds?.size ?: 0 val abandoned = buckets.firstOrNull { it.title == AbandonedTitle }?.workIds?.size ?: 0 buildList { add(works) if (inProgress &gt; 0) add("$inProgress in progress") if (abandoned &gt; 0) add("$abandoned abandoned") }.joinToString(" · ") }</code> | Android test: LibraryHistoryGroupingTests.stateTallyCountsInProgressAndAbandonedAndDropsZeros |
| `timeTallySaysTheOrderAndFandomSaysOnlyTheCount`<br>Time tally names the order; Fandom tally gives only the count. | `KudosTests/LibraryHistoryGroupingTests.swift:124–125` <code>#expect(LibraryHistoryGrouping.tallyLine(workCount: 1, grouping: .time, buckets: []) == "1 work · most recently read first")</code><br>`KudosTests/LibraryHistoryGroupingTests.swift:126–127` <code>#expect(LibraryHistoryGrouping.tallyLine(workCount: 6, grouping: .fandom, buckets: []) == "6 works")</code> | **kept** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/library/LibraryHistoryGrouping.kt:141–145` <code>fun tallyLine(workCount: Int, grouping: LibraryHistoryGrouping, buckets: List&lt;Bucket&gt;): String { val works = "$workCount ${if (workCount == 1) "work" else "works"}" return when (grouping) { Time, Flat -&gt; "$works · most recently read first" Fandom -&gt; works</code> | Android test: LibraryHistoryGroupingTests.timeTallySaysTheOrderAndFandomSaysOnlyTheCount |
| `finishedWinsOverEverythingIncludingAFreedFile`<br>Finished takes precedence even when its file is freed. | `KudosTests/LibraryHistoryGroupingTests.swift:132` <code>#expect(titles(.state, [finishedAndFreed]) == ["Finished"])</code> | **kept** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/library/LibraryHistoryGrouping.kt:109–116` <code>) { work -&gt; when { work.isFinished -&gt; FinishedTitle !work.hasEpub -&gt; ReadNotFinishedTitle !work.hasStartedReading -&gt; NotStartedTitle isAbandoned(work) -&gt; AbandonedTitle else -&gt; InProgressTitle }</code> | Android test: LibraryHistoryGroupingTests.finishedWinsOverEverythingIncludingAFreedFile |
| `fandomGroupsRankBySizeBecauseTheyHaveNoNaturalOrder`<br>Fandom groups sort by size then name, retaining No fandom. | `KudosTests/LibraryHistoryGroupingTests.swift:146` <code>#expect(titles(.fandom, works) == ["Naruto", "Dracula", "No fandom"])</code> | **kept** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/library/LibraryHistoryGrouping.kt:118–131` <code>Fandom -&gt; { val byFandom = linkedMapOf&lt;String, MutableList&lt;String&gt;&gt;() for (work in works) { // By family, so "Doctor Who" and "Doctor Who (2005)" are one group. val name = work.workFandoms.firstOrNull { it.isNotEmpty() } ?.let(FandomDisplayName::bareTitle) ?: NoFandomTitle byFandom.getOrPut(name) { mutableListOf() }.add(work.id) } byFandom.entries .sortedWith( compareByDescending&lt;Map.Entry&lt;String, MutableList&lt;String&gt;&gt;&gt; { it.value.size } .thenBy { it.key } ) .map { Bucket(it.key, it.value.toList()) }</code> | Android test: LibraryHistoryGroupingTests.fandomGroupsRankBySizeBecauseTheyHaveNoNaturalOrder |
| `everyWorkLandsInExactlyOneBucketUnderEveryGrouping`<br>Every input work belongs to exactly one bucket under each grouping. | `KudosTests/LibraryHistoryGroupingTests.swift:161` <code>#expect(ids.count == works.count, "\(grouping) dropped or duplicated a work")</code><br>`KudosTests/LibraryHistoryGroupingTests.swift:162` <code>#expect(Set(ids).count == works.count, "\(grouping) put a work in two buckets")</code> | **kept** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/library/LibraryHistoryGrouping.kt:98–131` <code>): List&lt;Bucket&gt; = when (grouping) { Flat -&gt; if (works.isEmpty()) emptyList() else listOf(Bucket(FlatTitle, works.map { it.id })) Time -&gt; ordered(TimeBucket.entries.map { it.title }, works) { TimeBucket.bucket(it.lastReadDate, now, zone, weekFields).title } // Five buckets, not three, because the reading state is a four-way partition and one of // the four (in progress) is split by Abandoned. Abandoned is checked before In progress: // it is a kind of in-progress, and a work in both would be counted twice in the tally. State -&gt; ordered( listOf(InProgressTitle, AbandonedTitle, ReadNotFinishedTitle, FinishedTitle, NotStartedTitle), works ) { work -&gt; when { work.isFinished -&gt; FinishedTitle !work.hasEpub -&gt; ReadNotFinishedTitle !work.hasStartedReading -&gt; NotStartedTitle isAbandoned(work) -&gt; AbandonedTitle else -&gt; InProgressTitle } } Fandom -&gt; { val byFandom = linkedMapOf&lt;String, MutableList&lt;String&gt;&gt;() for (work in works) { // By family, so "Doctor Who" and "Doctor Who (2005)" are one group. val name = work.workFandoms.firstOrNull { it.isNotEmpty() } ?.let(FandomDisplayName::bareTitle) ?: NoFandomTitle byFandom.getOrPut(name) { mutableListOf() }.add(work.id) } byFandom.entries .sortedWith( compareByDescending&lt;Map.Entry&lt;String, MutableList&lt;String&gt;&gt;&gt; { it.value.size } .thenBy { it.key } ) .map { Bucket(it.key, it.value.toList()) }</code><br>`android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/library/LibraryHistoryGrouping.kt:164–168` <code>val buckets = HashMap&lt;String, MutableList&lt;String&gt;&gt;() for (work in works) buckets.getOrPut(key(work)) { mutableListOf() }.add(work.id) return order.mapNotNull { title -&gt; buckets[title]?.takeIf { it.isNotEmpty() }?.let { Bucket(title, it.toList()) } }</code> | Android test: LibraryHistoryGroupingTests.everyWorkLandsInExactlyOneBucketUnderEveryGrouping |

## FavoriteQuickFilterTests

| Test — reader guarantee | iOS assertion quote | Verdict | Kotlin implementation quote | Consequence / Android test |
|---|---|---|---|---|

| `aNeverAskedQueueKeepsAndOnlyAnOnCollectionDoes`<br>A never-asked queue keeps copies; a collection keeps only when explicitly on. | `KudosTests/KeepOfflineAndHomeShelvesTests.swift:37` <code>#expect(KeepOffline.queueKeeps(nil))</code><br>`KudosTests/KeepOfflineAndHomeShelvesTests.swift:38` <code>#expect(KeepOffline.queueKeeps(true))</code><br>`KudosTests/KeepOfflineAndHomeShelvesTests.swift:39` <code>#expect(!KeepOffline.queueKeeps(false))</code><br>`KudosTests/KeepOfflineAndHomeShelvesTests.swift:40` <code>#expect(!KeepOffline.collectionKeeps(nil))</code><br>`KudosTests/KeepOfflineAndHomeShelvesTests.swift:41` <code>#expect(KeepOffline.collectionKeeps(true))</code><br>`KudosTests/KeepOfflineAndHomeShelvesTests.swift:42` <code>#expect(!KeepOffline.collectionKeeps(false))</code> | **kept** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/library/KeepOffline.kt:15–17` <code>object KeepOffline { /** A queue nobody has asked about (`null`) keeps, as queues always did. */ fun queueKeeps(value: Boolean?): Boolean = value != false</code><br>`android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/data/local/dao/WorkDao.kt:49–56` <code>WHERE reading_queues.keepsWorksOffline IS NULL OR reading_queues.keepsWorksOffline = 1 UNION SELECT DISTINCT collection_work_cross_refs.workId FROM collection_work_cross_refs INNER JOIN collections ON collections.id = collection_work_cross_refs.collectionId WHERE collections.keepsWorksOffline = 1</code> | Android test: WorkLifecycleTest.collectionNilDoesNotKeepButOnDoesAndClearsHold; keepOfflineQueueDrivesDownloadedQueryAndClearsHold |
| `finishingFreesOnlyWhatNoKeepOfflineContainerHolds`<br>Finishing holds an unprotected EPUB for 60 days and keeps container-protected copies without a hold. | `KudosTests/KeepOfflineAndHomeShelvesTests.swift:67` <code>#expect(!listed.isProtected)</code><br>`KudosTests/KeepOfflineAndHomeShelvesTests.swift:68` <code>#expect(collected.isProtected)</code><br>`KudosTests/KeepOfflineAndHomeShelvesTests.swift:72` <code>#expect(listed.hasEPUB &amp;&amp; listed.freedAt != nil)</code><br>`KudosTests/KeepOfflineAndHomeShelvesTests.swift:73` <code>#expect(collected.hasEPUB &amp;&amp; collected.freedAt == nil)</code><br>`KudosTests/KeepOfflineAndHomeShelvesTests.swift:80` <code>#expect(queued.isProtected)</code> | **kept** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/core/model/SavedWork.kt:117–119` <code>/** Last-copy and local-import protection stays independent of the Downloaded label. */ val isProtected: Boolean get() = isSaved &#124;&#124; isFavorite &#124;&#124; isKeptOffline &#124;&#124; !hasAo3WorkId &#124;&#124; ao3Unavailable</code><br>`android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/works/WorkRepository.kt:304–318` <code>suspend fun setFinished(workId: String, finished: Boolean): SavedWork? { val work = getWork(workId) ?: return null if (work.isFinished == finished) return work val now = clock() return if (finished) { upsert( work.copy( isFinished = true, freedAt = if (!work.isProtected &amp;&amp; work.hasEpub) work.freedAt ?: now else null, lastModifiedAt = now ) ) } else { upsert(work.copy(isFinished = false, freedAt = null, lastModifiedAt = now)) }</code><br>`android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/works/WorkRepository.kt:989` <code>val FREED_COPY_WINDOW: Duration = Duration.ofDays(60)</code> | This supersedes the old test name: the actual assertion requires a hold, not immediate freeing.<br>Android test: WorkLifecycleTest.markFinishedHoldsEpubWhenUnprotected; markFinishedKeepsEpubWhenProtected |

| `unreadMeansUnseenNewChapters`<br>Unread updates means unseen new chapters, not never opened. | `KudosTests/HomeUpdatePillTests.swift:15` <code>#expect(HomeUpdatePill.unread.apply(to: [updated, current]).map(\.title) == ["Updated"])</code><br>`KudosTests/HomeUpdatePillTests.swift:16` <code>#expect(HomeUpdatePill.all.apply(to: [updated, current]).count == 2)</code> | **kept** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/home/HomeSectionListScreen.kt:105–114` <code>private enum class HomeUpdatePill(val title: String) { All("All"), Unread("Unread"), Offline("Offline"); fun apply(items: List&lt;LibraryDisplayItem&gt;): List&lt;LibraryDisplayItem&gt; = when (this) { All -&gt; items Unread -&gt; items.filter { it.item.work.hasUpdate } Offline -&gt; items.filter { it.item.work.hasEpub } }</code><br>`android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/core/model/SavedWork.kt:153–157` <code>val hasUpdate: Boolean get() { val known = knownChapterCount ?: return false return known &gt; 0 &amp;&amp; postedChapterCount &gt; known }</code> | Android test: HomeFactsTest.updateFooterOnlySpeaksWhenThereIsAnUnseenChapter (hasUpdate predicate) |

| `softDeleteSetsFieldsAndKeepsEPUBOnDisk`<br>Soft deletion stamps a 90-day deadline and retains the EPUB. | `KudosTests/PreservedWorkTests.swift:25` <code>#expect(work.isPendingDeletion)</code><br>`KudosTests/PreservedWorkTests.swift:26` <code>#expect(work.deletedAt != nil)</code><br>`KudosTests/PreservedWorkTests.swift:27` <code>#expect(work.permanentDeletionScheduledAt != nil)</code><br>`KudosTests/PreservedWorkTests.swift:28` <code>#expect(FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: work.fileURL.path))</code> | **kept** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/works/WorkRepository.kt:384–395` <code>suspend fun softDelete(workId: String): SavedWork? { val work = getWork(workId) ?: return null val now = clock() val updated = work.copy( isDeleted = true, deletedAt = now, permanentDeletionScheduledAt = now.plus(RECOVERY_WINDOW), lastModifiedAt = now ) upsert(updated) recordWorkTombstone(updated, now, deletionReason = "workDeleted") return updated</code><br>`android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/works/WorkRepository.kt:986–987` <code>/** Apple `PreservedWorkService.recoveryWindow` — 90 days. */ val RECOVERY_WINDOW: Duration = Duration.ofDays(90)</code> | Android test: WorkLifecycleTest.softDeleteSetsFieldsKeepsEpubAndRecordsTombstone |

Canonicalize // both addresses, as iOS does, including old tombstones with chapter URLs. val held = tombstoneDao.getAll().map { it.toDomain() } val retainedIds = retractWorkTombstone(held, recordId, ao3WorkId, sourceUrl).mapTo(mutableSetOf()) { it.id } for (tombstone in held) { if (tombstone.id !in retainedIds) tombstoneDao.deleteById(tombstone.id) }</code> | Android test: WorkLifecycleTest.restoreFromRecentlyDeletedClearsFieldsAndRetractsTombstone |

## CollectionMembershipDeletionTests

| Test — reader guarantee | iOS assertion quote | Verdict | Kotlin implementation quote | Consequence / Android test |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `aMembershipDeletedElsewhereIsRemovedHere`<br>A trusted newer membership deletion removes a local collection link. | `KudosTests/CollectionMembershipDeletionTests.swift:109` <code>#expect(summary.removedCollectionMemberships == 1)</code><br>`KudosTests/CollectionMembershipDeletionTests.swift:110` <code>#expect(local.collection.works.isEmpty)</code><br>`KudosTests/CollectionMembershipDeletionTests.swift:111` <code>#expect(local.work.collections.isEmpty)</code> | **kept** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/backup/BackupMergeService.kt:1442–1461` <code>// iOS's second pass, in every mode but Replace: a work already in a collection here is // taken out by a trusted deletion record newer than the collection's last membership // change, unless this archive affirms it. Without it a removal made on another device // never arrived when File Merge only added, or when this device's copy of the // collection was the newer one, and the next export published the work again // (audit A23-3; queue memberships and annotations already have this pass). if (mode != BackupImportMode.REPLACE_LIBRARY) { collectionsById.entries.forEach { entry -&gt; // Only a collection this archive carries, as on iOS, where the pass belongs // to that collection's own restore. val affirmed = affirmedMemberships[entry.key] ?: return@forEach val collection = entry.value val changedAt = collection.lastMembershipChangedAt ?: collection.lastModifiedAt val kept = collection.workIds.filterNot { workId -&gt; BackupPaths.normalizeIdForComparison(workId) !in affirmed &amp;&amp; tombstoneIndex.collectionMembershipResolution( collectionMembershipRecordId(entry.key, workId), changedAt ) == TombstoneResolution.SUPPRESS_STALE } if (kept.size != collection.workIds.size) entry.setValue(collection.copy(workIds = kept))</code> | The second pass consults the local membership clock and does not require the incoming list to contain the deleted link.<br>Android test: BackupPhase2FieldsTest.aDeletionRecordRemovesAMembershipThatIsAlreadyHere |
| `aMembershipChosenAfterTheDeletionSurvives`<br>A deliberate local membership choice newer than a remote deletion survives. | `KudosTests/CollectionMembershipDeletionTests.swift:128` <code>#expect(summary.removedCollectionMemberships == 0)</code><br>`KudosTests/CollectionMembershipDeletionTests.swift:129` <code>#expect(local.collection.works.count == 1)</code> | **kept** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/backup/BackupMergeService.kt:1452–1459` <code>val affirmed = affirmedMemberships[entry.key] ?: return@forEach val collection = entry.value val changedAt = collection.lastMembershipChangedAt ?: collection.lastModifiedAt val kept = collection.workIds.filterNot { workId -&gt; BackupPaths.normalizeIdForComparison(workId) !in affirmed &amp;&amp; tombstoneIndex.collectionMembershipResolution( collectionMembershipRecordId(entry.key, workId), changedAt ) == TombstoneResolution.SUPPRESS_STALE</code> | The in-memory membership clock is honored. Android’s live stamping/export omissions are separate below.<br>Android test: BackupPhase2FieldsTest.aDeletionRecordRemovesAMembershipThatIsAlreadyHere (second scenario) |
| `replaceDoesNotRunTheSweep`<br>Replace does not run the collection-membership tombstone sweep. | `KudosTests/CollectionMembershipDeletionTests.swift:144` <code>#expect(summary.removedCollectionMemberships == 0)</code> | **kept** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/backup/BackupMergeService.kt:1448–1462` <code>if (mode != BackupImportMode.REPLACE_LIBRARY) { collectionsById.entries.forEach { entry -&gt; // Only a collection this archive carries, as on iOS, where the pass belongs // to that collection's own restore. val affirmed = affirmedMemberships[entry.key] ?: return@forEach val collection = entry.value val changedAt = collection.lastMembershipChangedAt ?: collection.lastModifiedAt val kept = collection.workIds.filterNot { workId -&gt; BackupPaths.normalizeIdForComparison(workId) !in affirmed &amp;&amp; tombstoneIndex.collectionMembershipResolution( collectionMembershipRecordId(entry.key, workId), changedAt ) == TombstoneResolution.SUPPRESS_STALE } if (kept.size != collection.workIds.size) entry.setValue(collection.copy(workIds = kept)) }</code> | Android test: no specific matching test located. |
| `aReAddTheArchiveVouchesForSurvivesTheSweep`<br>A newer archive-affirmed re-add survives the sweep in that same restore. | `KudosTests/CollectionMembershipDeletionTests.swift:191` <code>#expect(summary.removedCollectionMemberships == 0)</code><br>`KudosTests/CollectionMembershipDeletionTests.swift:192` <code>#expect(local.collection.works.count == 1)</code> | **kept** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/backup/BackupMergeService.kt:1322–1331` <code>// What this archive itself says is in the collection, and no deletion record // newer than the archive's copy contradicts (iOS `archiveAffirmedMemberships`). affirmedMemberships[id] = archived.workIDs .map { remapWorkId(it, workIdRemap) } .filterNot { workId -&gt; tombstoneIndex.collectionMembershipResolution( collectionMembershipRecordId(id, workId), incomingModified ) == TombstoneResolution.SUPPRESS_STALE } .mapTo(mutableSetOf()) { BackupPaths.normalizeIdForComparison(it) }</code><br>`android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/backup/BackupMergeService.kt:1452–1459` <code>val affirmed = affirmedMemberships[entry.key] ?: return@forEach val collection = entry.value val changedAt = collection.lastMembershipChangedAt ?: collection.lastModifiedAt val kept = collection.workIds.filterNot { workId -&gt; BackupPaths.normalizeIdForComparison(workId) !in affirmed &amp;&amp; tombstoneIndex.collectionMembershipResolution( collectionMembershipRecordId(entry.key, workId), changedAt ) == TombstoneResolution.SUPPRESS_STALE</code> | The archive’s own lastModifiedAt vouches for the re-add, before testing the older local clock.<br>Android test: BackupPhase2FieldsTest.aDeletionRecordRemovesAMembershipThatIsAlreadyHere (archive affirmation scenario) |
| `renamingACollectionDoesNotOverrideARemovalElsewhere`<br>Renaming/recoloring a collection cannot outrank a remote membership removal. | `KudosTests/CollectionMembershipDeletionTests.swift:214` <code>#expect(local.collection.lastMembershipChangedAt == chosenAt)</code><br>`KudosTests/CollectionMembershipDeletionTests.swift:221` <code>#expect(summary.removedCollectionMemberships == 1)</code><br>`KudosTests/CollectionMembershipDeletionTests.swift:222` <code>#expect(local.collection.works.isEmpty)</code> | **missing** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/works/WorkRepository.kt:820–823` <code>val updated = entity.copy(name = trimmed, lastModifiedAt = clock()) // @Upsert is an in-place UPDATE — memberships stay on the same row id. collectionDao.upsert(updated) return updated.toDomain(collectionDao.getActiveWorkIdsForCollection(updated.id))</code><br>`android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/works/WorkRepository.kt:835–839` <code>suspend fun addWorkToCollection(workId: String, collectionId: String): List&lt;WorkCollection&gt; { val entity = collectionDao.getById(collectionId) ?: return collectionsForWork(workId) if (entity.isDeleted) return collectionsForWork(workId) collectionDao.addWork(CollectionWorkCrossRef(collectionId, workId)) touchCollection(collectionId)</code><br>`android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/works/WorkRepository.kt:891–894` <code>private suspend fun touchCollection(collectionId: String) { val entity = collectionDao.getById(collectionId) ?: return collectionDao.upsert(entity.copy(lastModifiedAt = clock())) }</code><br>`android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/backup/BackupMergeService.kt:1452–1459` <code>val affirmed = affirmedMemberships[entry.key] ?: return@forEach val collection = entry.value val changedAt = collection.lastMembershipChangedAt ?: collection.lastModifiedAt val kept = collection.workIds.filterNot { workId -&gt; BackupPaths.normalizeIdForComparison(workId) !in affirmed &amp;&amp; tombstoneIndex.collectionMembershipResolution( collectionMembershipRecordId(entry.key, workId), changedAt ) == TombstoneResolution.SUPPRESS_STALE</code> | Live add/remove updates only lastModifiedAt, leaving lastMembershipChangedAt null. A later rename bumps that same fallback clock, making an older remote removal look stale. The local membership can be republished instead of removed.<br>Android test: no specific matching test located. |

| `candidatesExcludeExistingMembers`<br>The collection picker offers live outsiders while excluding current members. | `KudosTests/CollectionWorkPickerTests.swift:21` <code>#expect(candidates.map(\.id) == [outsider.id])</code> | **missing** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/library/CollectionDetailScreen.kt:612–620` <code>if (showAddPicker) { val allSavedWorks by workRepository.observeSavedWorks().collectAsState(initial = emptyList()) val currentWorkIds = works.map { it.id }.toSet() val availableToAdd = allSavedWorks.filter { it.id !in currentWorkIds } AddLibraryWorksSheet( destinationName = collection?.name.orEmpty(), scopeName = "collection", candidates = availableToAdd,</code><br>`android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/works/WorkRepository.kt:60–63` <code>fun observeSavedWorks(): Flow&lt;List&lt;SavedWork&gt;&gt; { return workDao.observeAll().decorated().map { works -&gt; works.filter { it.isProtected &amp;&amp; !it.isQueueOnlyWork } }.distinctUntilChanged()</code><br>`android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/core/model/SavedWork.kt:117–126` <code>/** Last-copy and local-import protection stays independent of the Downloaded label. */ val isProtected: Boolean get() = isSaved &#124;&#124; isFavorite &#124;&#124; isKeptOffline &#124;&#124; !hasAo3WorkId &#124;&#124; ao3Unavailable val needsAO3Refresh: Boolean get() = (System.currentTimeMillis() - (lastTagRefreshAttemptAt?.toEpochMilli() ?: 0)) &gt; 86400000L /** iOS `SavedWork.isQueueOnlyWork`: queued but not otherwise protected. */ val isQueueOnlyWork: Boolean get() = isQueuedForLater &amp;&amp; !isSaved &amp;&amp; !isFavorite</code> | The existing-member/queue-only exclusions are right, but the candidate source is observeSavedWorks, which also requires isProtected. trimmed != queue.name) { require(queue.kindRaw != ReadingQueueKind.SAVED_FOR_LATER) { "Cannot rename system queues." } name = trimmed changed = true } var hue = queue.hue var colorHex = queue.colorHex if (queue.hue != edit.hue &#124;&#124; queue.colorHex != edit.colorHex) { hue = edit.hue colorHex = edit.colorHex changed = true } val wasKept = queue.keepsWorksOffline != false var keeps = queue.keepsWorksOffline if (edit.keepsWorksOffline != wasKept) { keeps = edit.keepsWorksOffline changed = true } var notes = queue.notes if ((edit.notes ?: "") != (queue.notes ?: "")) { notes = edit.notes changed = true } if (changed) { queueDao.upsertQueue( queue.copy( name = name, hue = hue, colorHex = colorHex, keepsWorksOffline = keeps, notes = notes, dateUpdated = clock() ) ) } replaceQueueTags(queueId, edit.tagNames) val turnedOn = edit.keepsWorksOffline &amp;&amp; MINIMUM_PERSISTABLE_SECONDS) return dao.upsertSession( ReadingSessionEntity( id = session.recordId, workID = session.workId, ao3WorkID = work.ao3WorkID ?: session.ao3WorkId, sourceURL = work.sourceUrl.ifBlank { session.sourceUrl }, workTitle = work.title.ifBlank { session.workTitle }, startedAt = session.startedAt, endedAt = now, durationSeconds = session.accumulatedSeconds.coerceAtLeast(0.0), lastSpineIndex = work.lastSpineIndex, chapterTitle = locatorTitle(work.readiumLocator), endingProgress = (work.readingProgress ?: 0.0).coerceIn(0.0, 1.0), wordCount = work.wordCount, chapterCountAtVisit = work.postedChapterCount, didFinish = session.didFinish, lastModifiedAt = now ) )</code><br>`android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/reader/ReadingLogService.kt:133–135` <code>companion object { const val MINIMUM_PERSISTABLE_SECONDS = 15.0 private val json = Json { ignoreUnknownKeys = true }</code> | Android test: ReadingLogServiceTest.startThenEndPersistsAllIosFields; didFinish(current, work) ) openSessions[work.id] = session persist(session, work, now) } suspend fun resumeSession(workId: String): Unit = mutex.withLock { val current = openSessions[workId] ?: return@withLock if (!current.isPaused) return@withLock openSessions[workId] = current.copy(lastResumedAt = clock(), isPaused = false) } suspend fun endSession(work: SavedWork): Unit = mutex.withLock { val current = openSessions.remove(work.id) ?: return@withLock val now = clock() val session = current.copy( accumulatedSeconds = current.accumulatedSeconds + if (current.isPaused) { 0.0 } else { elapsed(current.lastResumedAt, now) }, didFinish = current.didFinish &#124;&#124; didFinish(current, work) ) persist(session, work, now)</code><br>`android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/reader/ReadingLogService.kt:94–103` <code>dao.upsertSession( ReadingSessionEntity( id = session.recordId, workID = session.workId, ao3WorkID = work.ao3WorkID ?: session.ao3WorkId, sourceURL = work.sourceUrl.ifBlank { session.sourceUrl }, workTitle = work.title.ifBlank { session.workTitle }, startedAt = session.startedAt, endedAt = now, durationSeconds = session.accumulatedSeconds.coerceAtLeast(0.0),</code> | The stable recordId is reused by Room upsert.<br>Android test: ReadingLogServiceTest.resumedVisitStaysOneRowAndDoesNotCountBackgroundTime |

## ReadingStatisticsTests

| Test — reader guarantee | iOS assertion quote | Verdict | Kotlin implementation quote | Consequence / Android test |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `separatesStartedFinishedAndInProgressWorks`<br>Totals distinguish unread, started, finished, and in-progress works with the right completion denominator. | `KudosTests/ReadingStatisticsTests.swift:16` <code>#expect(statistics.totalWorks == 3)</code><br>`KudosTests/ReadingStatisticsTests.swift:17` <code>#expect(statistics.startedWorks == 2)</code><br>`KudosTests/ReadingStatisticsTests.swift:18` <code>#expect(statistics.finishedWorks == 1)</code><br>`KudosTests/ReadingStatisticsTests.swift:19` <code>#expect(statistics.inProgressWorks == 1)</code><br>`KudosTests/ReadingStatisticsTests.swift:20` <code>#expect(statistics.completionRate == 0.5)</code> | **kept** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/library/ReadingStatistics.kt:29–34` <code>val completionRate: Double get() = if (startedWorks &gt; 0) { finishedWorks.toDouble() / startedWorks.toDouble() } else { 0.0 }</code><br>`android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/library/ReadingStatistics.kt:42–63` <code>val started = works.filter(::hasStarted) val finished = works.filter { it.isFinished } val startOfToday = ZonedDateTime.ofInstant(now, zone).toLocalDate() val sevenDayStart = startOfDay(startOfToday.minusDays(6), zone) val thirtyDayStart = startOfDay(startOfToday.minusDays(29), zone) return ReadingStatistics( totalWorks = works.size, startedWorks = started.size, finishedWorks = finished.size, inProgressWorks = started.count { !it.isFinished }, wordsRead = finished.sumOf { maxOf(0, it.wordCount) }, openedLast7Days = countOpened(works, sevenDayStart, now), openedLast30Days = countOpened(works, thirtyDayStart, now), latestReadDate = works.mapNotNull { it.lastReadDate }.maxOrNull(), topFandoms = fandomCounts(started) ) } /** Finished counts as started even if its progress fields were cleared. */ fun hasStarted(work: SavedWork): Boolean = work.isFinished &#124;&#124; work.hasStartedReading</code> | Android test: ReadingStatisticsTest.separatesStartedFinishedAndInProgressWorks |
| `wordsReadCountsOnlyFinishedWorksWithKnownTotals`<br>Words read sums only finished works with known positive totals. | `KudosTests/ReadingStatisticsTests.swift:37` <code>#expect(statistics.wordsRead == 12_500)</code> | **kept** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/library/ReadingStatistics.kt:43–54` <code>val finished = works.filter { it.isFinished } val startOfToday = ZonedDateTime.ofInstant(now, zone).toLocalDate() val sevenDayStart = startOfDay(startOfToday.minusDays(6), zone) val thirtyDayStart = startOfDay(startOfToday.minusDays(29), zone) return ReadingStatistics( totalWorks = works.size, startedWorks = started.size, finishedWorks = finished.size, inProgressWorks = started.count { !it.isFinished }, wordsRead = finished.sumOf { maxOf(0, it.wordCount) },</code> | Android test: ReadingStatisticsTest.wordsReadCountsOnlyFinishedWorksWithKnownTotals |
| `recentActivityUsesDistinctWorksAndCalendarDays`<br>Recent activity counts works within local calendar-day windows and reports the newest visit. | `KudosTests/ReadingStatisticsTests.swift:62` <code>#expect(statistics.openedLast7Days == 2)</code><br>`KudosTests/ReadingStatisticsTests.swift:63` <code>#expect(statistics.openedLast30Days == 3)</code><br>`KudosTests/ReadingStatisticsTests.swift:64` <code>#expect(statistics.latestReadDate == now)</code> | **kept** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/library/ReadingStatistics.kt:45–57` <code>val startOfToday = ZonedDateTime.ofInstant(now, zone).toLocalDate() val sevenDayStart = startOfDay(startOfToday.minusDays(6), zone) val thirtyDayStart = startOfDay(startOfToday.minusDays(29), zone) return ReadingStatistics( totalWorks = works.size, startedWorks = started.size, finishedWorks = finished.size, inProgressWorks = started.count { !it.isFinished }, wordsRead = finished.sumOf { maxOf(0, it.wordCount) }, openedLast7Days = countOpened(works, sevenDayStart, now), openedLast30Days = countOpened(works, thirtyDayStart, now), latestReadDate = works.mapNotNull { it.lastReadDate }.maxOrNull(),</code><br>`android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/library/ReadingStatistics.kt:65–72` <code>private fun countOpened( works: List&lt;SavedWork&gt;, since: Instant, through: Instant ): Int = works.count { work -&gt; val date = work.lastReadDate ?: return@count false !date.isBefore(since) &amp;&amp; !date.isAfter(through) }</code> | Android test: ReadingStatisticsTest.recentActivityUsesDistinctWorksAndCalendarDays |
| `readiumOnlyWorkCountsAsStarted`<br>A stored Readium locator alone counts as started/in progress. | `KudosTests/ReadingStatisticsTests.swift:76` <code>#expect(statistics.startedWorks == 1)</code><br>`KudosTests/ReadingStatisticsTests.swift:77` <code>#expect(statistics.inProgressWorks == 1)</code> | **kept** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/library/ReadingStatistics.kt:63` <code>fun hasStarted(work: SavedWork): Boolean = work.isFinished &#124;&#124; work.hasStartedReading</code><br>`android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/core/model/SavedWork.kt:128–135` <code>val hasStartedReading: Boolean get() = lastReadDate != null &#124;&#124; !readiumLocator.isNullOrBlank() &#124;&#124; lastSpineIndex &gt; 0 &#124;&#124; lastScrollFraction &gt; 0.0 val isInProgress: Boolean get() = hasEpub &amp;&amp; !isFinished &amp;&amp; hasStartedReading</code> | Android test: ReadingStatisticsTest.readiumOnlyWorkCountsAsStarted |
| `topFandomsCountEachFandomOncePerStartedWork`<br>Top fandom counts each fandom at most once per started work. | `KudosTests/ReadingStatisticsTests.swift:94–97` <code>#expect(statistics.topFandoms == [ .init(name: "Naruto", count: 2), .init(name: "Bleach", count: 1), ])</code> | **kept** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/library/ReadingStatistics.kt:74–89` <code>private fun fandomCounts(works: List&lt;SavedWork&gt;): List&lt;FandomCount&gt; { val counts = mutableMapOf&lt;String, Int&gt;() for (work in works) { val uniqueFandoms = work.workFandoms .map { it.trim() } .filter { it.isNotEmpty() } .toSet() for (fandom in uniqueFandoms) { counts[fandom] = (counts[fandom] ?: 0) + 1 } } return counts.map { (name, count) -&gt; FandomCount(name, count) } .sortedWith( compareByDescending&lt;FandomCount&gt; { it.count } .thenBy(String.CASE_INSENSITIVE_ORDER) { it.name } )</code> | Android test: ReadingStatisticsTest.topFandomsCountEachFandomOncePerStartedWork |

## ReadingInsightsTests

| Test — reader guarantee | iOS assertion quote | Verdict | Kotlin implementation quote | Consequence / Android test |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `theTypicalSessionIsTheMedianSoOneBingeCannotMoveIt`<br>Typical session is the median, so a single binge cannot skew it like a mean. | `KudosTests/ReadingInsightsTests.swift:57` <code>#expect(ReadingInsights.medianSeconds(of: facts) == 35 * 60)</code> | **kept** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/library/ReadingStatistics.kt:228–237` <code>fun medianSeconds(facts: List&lt;ReadingSessionFacts&gt;): Double { val durations = facts.map { it.durationSeconds }.filter { it &gt; 0 }.sorted() if (durations.isEmpty()) return 0.0 val middle = durations.size / 2 return if (durations.size % 2 == 0) { (durations[middle - 1] + durations[middle]) / 2 } else { durations[middle] } }</code> | Android test: ReadingStatisticsTest.theTypicalSessionIsTheMedianSoOneBingeCannotMoveIt |
| `medianOfNothingIsZeroRatherThanACrash`<br>No sessions yields median zero safely. | `KudosTests/ReadingInsightsTests.swift:61` <code>#expect(ReadingInsights.medianSeconds(of: []) == 0)</code> | **kept** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/library/ReadingStatistics.kt:228–231` <code>fun medianSeconds(facts: List&lt;ReadingSessionFacts&gt;): Double { val durations = facts.map { it.durationSeconds }.filter { it &gt; 0 }.sorted() if (durations.isEmpty()) return 0.0 val middle = durations.size / 2</code> | Android test: ReadingStatisticsTest.medianOfNothingIsZeroRatherThanACrash |
| `finishRateCountsWorksNotSessionsSoARereadCannotInflateIt`<br>Finish rate uses distinct work IDs so rereads cannot inflate it. | `KudosTests/ReadingInsightsTests.swift:76` <code>#expect(ReadingInsights.finishRate(of: facts) == 0.5)</code> | **kept** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/library/ReadingStatistics.kt:246–250` <code>fun finishRate(facts: List&lt;ReadingSessionFacts&gt;): Double? { val startedWorkIDs = facts.map { it.workID }.toSet() if (startedWorkIDs.isEmpty()) return null val finishedWorkIDs = facts.filter { it.didFinish }.map { it.workID }.toSet() return finishedWorkIDs.size.toDouble() / startedWorkIDs.size.toDouble()</code> | Android test: ReadingStatisticsTest.finishRateCountsWorksNotSessionsSoARereadCannotInflateIt |
| `noStartedWorksIsNoDataRatherThanZeroPercent`<br>No started works yields no finish-rate data, not 0%. | `KudosTests/ReadingInsightsTests.swift:80` <code>#expect(ReadingInsights.finishRate(of: []) == nil)</code> | **kept** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/library/ReadingStatistics.kt:246–248` <code>fun finishRate(facts: List&lt;ReadingSessionFacts&gt;): Double? { val startedWorkIDs = facts.map { it.workID }.toSet() if (startedWorkIDs.isEmpty()) return null</code> | Android test: ReadingStatisticsTest.noStartedWorksIsNoDataRatherThanZeroPercent |
| `aStreakCountsDaysSoTwoSessionsInOneEveningAreOneDay`<br>A reading streak counts distinct consecutive calendar days. | `KudosTests/ReadingInsightsTests.swift:94` <code>#expect(ReadingInsights.longestStreak(of: facts, calendar: calendar) == 3)</code> | **kept** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/library/ReadingStatistics.kt:253–269` <code>fun longestStreak( facts: List&lt;ReadingSessionFacts&gt;, zone: ZoneId ): Int { val days = facts.map { it.startedAt.atZone(zone).toLocalDate() }.toSortedSet() if (days.isEmpty()) return 0 var longest = 1 var current = 1 days.zipWithNext().forEach { (previous, day) -&gt; if (previous.plusDays(1) == day) { current += 1 longest = maxOf(longest, current) } else { current = 1 } } return longest</code> | Android test: ReadingStatisticsTest.aStreakCountsDaysSoTwoSessionsInOneEveningAreOneDay |
| `aStreakOfOneDayIsOneNotZero`<br>One reading day is a streak of one. | `KudosTests/ReadingInsightsTests.swift:98–99` <code>#expect(ReadingInsights.longestStreak(of: [fact(on: 4, minutes: 30)], calendar: calendar) == 1)</code> | **kept** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/library/ReadingStatistics.kt:257–260` <code>val days = facts.map { it.startedAt.atZone(zone).toLocalDate() }.toSortedSet() if (days.isEmpty()) return 0 var longest = 1 var current = 1</code> | Android test: ReadingStatisticsTest.aStreakOfOneDayIsOneNotZero |
| `fandomSharesPartitionTheHoursRatherThanOverCounting`<br>Fandom shares partition all recorded hours, folding unnamed/extra fandoms into the remainder. | `KudosTests/ReadingInsightsTests.swift:115–117` <code>#expect(shares.map(\.name) == [ "Naruto", "Cyberpunk 2077", "Blade Runner", "Everything else" ])</code><br>`KudosTests/ReadingInsightsTests.swift:121` <code>#expect(shares.last?.seconds == Double(15 * 60))</code><br>`KudosTests/ReadingInsightsTests.swift:122` <code>#expect(shares.last?.isRemainder == true)</code><br>`KudosTests/ReadingInsightsTests.swift:123` <code>#expect(shares.reduce(0) { $0 + $1.seconds } == 125 * 60)</code> | **kept** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/library/ReadingStatistics.kt:208–225` <code>fun fandomShares(facts: List&lt;ReadingSessionFacts&gt;): List&lt;FandomShare&gt; { val seconds = mutableMapOf&lt;String, Double&gt;() var unattributed = 0.0 for (fact in facts) { if (fact.fandom.isEmpty()) { unattributed += fact.durationSeconds } else { seconds[fact.fandom] = (seconds[fact.fandom] ?: 0.0) + fact.durationSeconds } } val ranked = seconds.entries.sortedWith( compareByDescending&lt;Map.Entry&lt;String, Double&gt;&gt; { it.value }.thenBy { it.key } ) val shares = ranked.take(namedFandomLimit).map { FandomShare(it.key, it.value) } .toMutableList() val remainder = ranked.drop(namedFandomLimit).sumOf { it.value } + unattributed if (remainder &gt; 0) shares += FandomShare(remainderName, remainder, true) return shares</code> | Android test: ReadingStatisticsTest.fandomSharesPartitionTheHoursRatherThanOverCounting |
| `noRemainderRowAppearsWhenNothingIsLeftOver`<br>No remainder row appears when all time is named in the top fandoms. | `KudosTests/ReadingInsightsTests.swift:131` <code>#expect(shares.map(\.name) == ["Naruto", "Dracula"])</code><br>`KudosTests/ReadingInsightsTests.swift:132` <code>#expect(shares.contains { $0.isRemainder } == false)</code> | **kept** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/library/ReadingStatistics.kt:221–225` <code>val shares = ranked.take(namedFandomLimit).map { FandomShare(it.key, it.value) } .toMutableList() val remainder = ranked.drop(namedFandomLimit).sumOf { it.value } + unattributed if (remainder &gt; 0) shares += FandomShare(remainderName, remainder, true) return shares</code> | Android test: ReadingStatisticsTest.noRemainderRowAppearsWhenNothingIsLeftOver |
| `wordsPerHourIsWeightedByTimeNotAveragedAcrossSessions`<br>Words/hour divides total words by total positive session time. | `KudosTests/ReadingInsightsTests.swift:146` <code>#expect(ReadingInsights.wordsPerHour(of: facts) == 17_500)</code> | **kept** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/library/ReadingStatistics.kt:239–244` <code>fun wordsPerHour(facts: List&lt;ReadingSessionFacts&gt;): Double { val usable = facts.filter { it.durationSeconds &gt; 0 } val totalSeconds = usable.sumOf { it.durationSeconds } if (totalSeconds &lt;= 0) return 0.0 return usable.sumOf { it.wordCount }.toDouble() / (totalSeconds / 3_600) }</code> | Android test: ReadingStatisticsTest.wordsPerHourIsWeightedByTimeNotAveragedAcrossSessions |
| `aFirstPeriodHasNoDeltaRatherThanADeltaAgainstZero`<br>A first period has no invented delta against zero. | `KudosTests/ReadingInsightsTests.swift:155` <code>#expect(insights.previousPeriodSeconds == nil)</code><br>`KudosTests/ReadingInsightsTests.swift:156` <code>#expect(insights.totalSeconds == 3_600)</code> | **kept** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/library/ReadingStatistics.kt:177–185` <code>fun make( facts: List&lt;ReadingSessionFacts&gt;, previousPeriodFacts: List&lt;ReadingSessionFacts&gt; = emptyList(), zone: ZoneId = ZoneId.systemDefault() ): ReadingInsights = ReadingInsights( totalSeconds = facts.sumOf { it.durationSeconds }, previousPeriodSeconds = previousPeriodFacts.takeIf { it.isNotEmpty() } ?.sumOf { it.durationSeconds }, weeklySeconds = weeklyBuckets(facts, zone),</code> | Android test: ReadingStatisticsTest.aFirstPeriodHasNoDeltaRatherThanADeltaAgainstZero |
| `theDeltaComesFromThePriorPeriodsOwnRows`<br>A delta compares with the previous period’s own rows. | `KudosTests/ReadingInsightsTests.swift:165` <code>#expect(insights.previousPeriodSeconds == 1_800)</code><br>`KudosTests/ReadingInsightsTests.swift:166–168` <code>#expect(ReadingInsights.signedHoursLabel( insights.totalSeconds - (insights.previousPeriodSeconds ?? 0) ) == "+1.0")</code> | **kept** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/library/ReadingStatistics.kt:177–185` <code>fun make( facts: List&lt;ReadingSessionFacts&gt;, previousPeriodFacts: List&lt;ReadingSessionFacts&gt; = emptyList(), zone: ZoneId = ZoneId.systemDefault() ): ReadingInsights = ReadingInsights( totalSeconds = facts.sumOf { it.durationSeconds }, previousPeriodSeconds = previousPeriodFacts.takeIf { it.isNotEmpty() } ?.sumOf { it.durationSeconds }, weeklySeconds = weeklyBuckets(facts, zone),</code><br>`android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/library/ReadingStatistics.kt:275–278` <code>fun signedHoursLabel(deltaSeconds: Double): String { val hours = deltaSeconds / 3_600 return (if (hours &lt; 0) "−" else "+") + String.format(Locale.US, "%.1f", abs(hours))</code> | Android test: ReadingStatisticsTest.theDeltaComesFromThePriorPeriodsOwnRows |
| `labelsMatchTheSpecsOwnFormats`<br>Hours, signed delta, and duration labels match the tested formats. | `KudosTests/ReadingInsightsTests.swift:174` <code>#expect(ReadingInsights.hoursLabel(18.4 * 3600) == "18.4")</code><br>`KudosTests/ReadingInsightsTests.swift:175` <code>#expect(ReadingInsights.signedHoursLabel(-0.6 * 3600) == "−0.6")</code><br>`KudosTests/ReadingInsightsTests.swift:176` <code>#expect(ReadingInsights.durationLabel(31 * 60) == "31 min")</code><br>`KudosTests/ReadingInsightsTests.swift:177` <code>#expect(ReadingInsights.durationLabel(72 * 60) == "1h 12m")</code><br>`KudosTests/ReadingInsightsTests.swift:178` <code>#expect(ReadingInsights.durationLabel(120 * 60) == "2h")</code> | **kept** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/library/ReadingStatistics.kt:272–286` <code>fun hoursLabel(seconds: Double): String = String.format(Locale.US, "%.1f", maxOf(0.0, seconds) / 3_600) fun signedHoursLabel(deltaSeconds: Double): String { val hours = deltaSeconds / 3_600 return (if (hours &lt; 0) "−" else "+") + String.format(Locale.US, "%.1f", abs(hours)) } fun durationLabel(seconds: Double): String { val totalMinutes = (maxOf(0.0, seconds) / 60).roundToInt() if (totalMinutes &lt; 60) return "$totalMinutes min" val hours = totalMinutes / 60 val minutes = totalMinutes % 60 return if (minutes == 0) "${hours}h" else "${hours}h ${minutes}m"</code> | Android test: ReadingStatisticsTest.labelsMatchTheSpecsOwnFormats |

## ReadingAffinitiesTests

| Test — reader guarantee | iOS assertion quote | Verdict | Kotlin implementation quote | Consequence / Android test |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `onlyNamesWithSomethingReadBehindThemAppear`<br>Only authors with reading evidence appear as reading affinities. | `KudosTests/ReadingAffinitiesTests.swift:45` <code>#expect(rows.map(\.name) == ["kestrelmoon"])</code> | **kept** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/library/ReadingAffinities.kt:105–112` <code>val summary = summaries[work.id] val wasRead = (summary?.visitCount ?: 0) &gt; 0 &#124;&#124; work.hasStartedReading val unread = !work.hasStartedReading &amp;&amp; summary == null for (name in keys(work).map { it.trim() }.filter { it.isNotEmpty() }) { val row = byName[name] ?: Row(name) val date = if (wasRead) summary?.lastEndedAt ?: work.lastReadDate else null byName[name] = row.copy( worksRead = row.worksRead + if (wasRead) 1 else 0,</code><br>`android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/library/ReadingAffinities.kt:128–129` <code>return byName.values.filter { it.worksRead &gt; 0 } .sortedWith(ranking.thenBy(String.CASE_INSENSITIVE_ORDER) { it.name })</code> | Android test: ReadingAffinitiesTests.onlyNamesWithSomethingReadBehindThemAppear |
| `onlyASingleRegisteredAuthorGivesABylineAUsername`<br>Only a single registered author gives a byline a profile username. | `KudosTests/ReadingAffinitiesTests.swift:62` <code>#expect(rows.first { $0.name == "kestrelmoon" }?.username == "kestrelmoon")</code><br>`KudosTests/ReadingAffinitiesTests.swift:63` <code>#expect(rows.first { $0.name == "alpha, beta" }?.username == nil)</code> | **kept** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/library/ReadingAffinities.kt:65–75` <code>fun authors(works: List&lt;SavedWork&gt;, summaries: Map&lt;String, WorkReadingSummary&gt;, order: Order = Order.Recent, savedForLaterIds: Set&lt;String&gt; = emptySet()): List&lt;Row&gt; { val usernames = mutableMapOf&lt;String, String&gt;() for (work in works) { val byline = work.author.trim() if (byline.isEmpty() &#124;&#124; byline in usernames) continue (singleRegisteredUsername(work.authorIdentitiesJSON) ?: accountFromAO3Byline(work)) ?.let { usernames[byline] = it } } return rows(works, summaries, order, savedForLaterIds) { listOf(it.author) } .map { it.copy(username = usernames[it.name]) }</code><br>`android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/library/ReadingAffinities.kt:153–158` <code>private fun singleRegisteredUsername(raw: String): String? { val identities = runCatching { Json.parseToJsonElement(raw) as? JsonArray }.getOrNull() ?: return null val registered = identities.filter { (it as? JsonObject)?.get("kind") == JsonPrimitive("registered") } val identity = registered.singleOrNull() as? JsonObject ?: return null return (identity["username"] as? JsonPrimitive)?.takeIf { it.isString } ?.contentOrNull?.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() }</code> | Android test: ReadingAffinitiesTests.onlyASingleRegisteredAuthorGivesABylineAUsername |
| `aWorkWithTwoTagsIsCountedByBothRatherThanSplit`<br>A two-tag work contributes one work to each tag’s count. | `KudosTests/ReadingAffinitiesTests.swift:72` <code>#expect(rows.count == 2)</code><br>`KudosTests/ReadingAffinitiesTests.swift:73` <code>#expect(rows.allSatisfy { $0.worksRead == 1 })</code> | **kept** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/library/ReadingAffinities.kt:82–84` <code>fun tags(works: List&lt;SavedWork&gt;, summaries: Map&lt;String, WorkReadingSummary&gt;, order: Order = Order.Recent, savedForLaterIds: Set&lt;String&gt; = emptySet()): List&lt;Row&gt; = rows(works, summaries, order, savedForLaterIds) { it.workFreeforms.ifEmpty { it.workTags } }</code><br>`android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/library/ReadingAffinities.kt:108–119` <code>for (name in keys(work).map { it.trim() }.filter { it.isNotEmpty() }) { val row = byName[name] ?: Row(name) val date = if (wasRead) summary?.lastEndedAt ?: work.lastReadDate else null byName[name] = row.copy( worksRead = row.worksRead + if (wasRead) 1 else 0, totalSeconds = row.totalSeconds + if (wasRead) summary?.totalSeconds ?: 0.0 else 0.0, lastRead = listOfNotNull(row.lastRead, date).maxOrNull(), unreadInLibrary = row.unreadInLibrary + if (unread) 1 else 0, downloadedInLibrary = row.downloadedInLibrary + if (unread &amp;&amp; work.isDownloaded) 1 else 0, savedForLater = row.savedForLater + if (unread &amp;&amp; (work.id in savedForLaterIds &#124;&#124; (work.isSaved &amp;&amp; !work.isQueuedForLater))) 1 else 0, favorited = row.favorited + if (work.isFavorite) 1 else 0</code> | Android test: ReadingAffinitiesTests.aWorkWithTwoTagsIsCountedByBothRatherThanSplit |
| `unreadCountsSplitOutDownloadsAndSavedForLater`<br>Unread library totals split downloaded copies from Saved for Later entries. | `KudosTests/ReadingAffinitiesTests.swift:87` <code>#expect(rows.count == 1)</code><br>`KudosTests/ReadingAffinitiesTests.swift:88` <code>#expect(rows.first?.worksRead == 1)</code><br>`KudosTests/ReadingAffinitiesTests.swift:89` <code>#expect(rows.first?.unreadInLibrary == 2)</code><br>`KudosTests/ReadingAffinitiesTests.swift:90` <code>#expect(rows.first?.downloadedInLibrary == 1)</code><br>`KudosTests/ReadingAffinitiesTests.swift:91` <code>#expect(rows.first?.savedForLater == 1)</code> | **kept** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/library/ReadingAffinities.kt:105–118` <code>val summary = summaries[work.id] val wasRead = (summary?.visitCount ?: 0) &gt; 0 &#124;&#124; work.hasStartedReading val unread = !work.hasStartedReading &amp;&amp; summary == null for (name in keys(work).map { it.trim() }.filter { it.isNotEmpty() }) { val row = byName[name] ?: Row(name) val date = if (wasRead) summary?.lastEndedAt ?: work.lastReadDate else null byName[name] = row.copy( worksRead = row.worksRead + if (wasRead) 1 else 0, totalSeconds = row.totalSeconds + if (wasRead) summary?.totalSeconds ?: 0.0 else 0.0, lastRead = listOfNotNull(row.lastRead, date).maxOrNull(), unreadInLibrary = row.unreadInLibrary + if (unread) 1 else 0, downloadedInLibrary = row.downloadedInLibrary + if (unread &amp;&amp; work.isDownloaded) 1 else 0, savedForLater = row.savedForLater + if (unread &amp;&amp; (work.id in savedForLaterIds &#124;&#124; (work.isSaved &amp;&amp; !work.isQueuedForLater))) 1 else 0,</code> | The fixture’s blank-source downloaded work is a kept local import. The isSaved-only entry is recognized as legacy Saved for Later.<br>Android test: ReadingAffinitiesTests.unreadCountsSplitOutDownloadsAndSavedForLater |

| `usesPersistedPublicationLabelsIncludingFrontMatter`<br>Position labels retain actual publication titles, including Preface and trimmed Afterword. | `KudosTests/WorkReadingPositionTests.swift:7–9` <code>#expect(WorkReadingPosition.title( from: #"{"title":"Chapter 3: Begin Again","locations":{"totalProgression":0.42}}"# ) == "Chapter 3: Begin Again")</code><br>`KudosTests/WorkReadingPositionTests.swift:10` <code>#expect(WorkReadingPosition.title(from: #"{"title":"Preface"}"#) == "Preface")</code><br>`KudosTests/WorkReadingPositionTests.swift:11` <code>#expect(WorkReadingPosition.title(from: #"{"title":" Afterword "}"#) == "Afterword")</code> | **kept** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/home/HomeFacts.kt:116–141` <code>/** Readium locator JSON `title`, or null. A small scan so unit tests need no Android JSON stub. */ fun locatorTitle(locatorJson: String?): String? { if (locatorJson.isNullOrBlank()) return null val key = "\"title\"" val index = locatorJson.indexOf(key) if (index &lt; 0) return null var cursor = index + key.length while (cursor &lt; locatorJson.length &amp;&amp; locatorJson[cursor].isWhitespace()) cursor++ if (cursor &gt;= locatorJson.length &#124;&#124; locatorJson[cursor] != ':') return null cursor++ while (cursor &lt; locatorJson.length &amp;&amp; locatorJson[cursor].isWhitespace()) cursor++ if (cursor &gt;= locatorJson.length &#124;&#124; locatorJson[cursor] != '"') return null cursor++ val buffer = StringBuilder() while (cursor &lt; locatorJson.length) { val character = locatorJson[cursor] if (character == '\\' &amp;&amp; cursor + 1 &lt; locatorJson.length) { buffer.append(locatorJson[cursor + 1]) cursor += 2 continue } if (character == '"') break buffer.append(character) cursor++ } return buffer.toString().trim().ifEmpty { null }</code> | Android test: HomeFactsTest.locatorTitleReadsTheJsonField |
| `missingOrInvalidLocatorLabelUsesTheFallback`<br>The tested missing/invalid labels return no title so callers use their fallback. | `KudosTests/WorkReadingPositionTests.swift:16` <code>#expect(WorkReadingPosition.title(from: locatorJSON) == nil)</code> | **kept** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/home/HomeFacts.kt:117–128` <code>fun locatorTitle(locatorJson: String?): String? { if (locatorJson.isNullOrBlank()) return null val key = "\"title\"" val index = locatorJson.indexOf(key) if (index &lt; 0) return null var cursor = index + key.length while (cursor &lt; locatorJson.length &amp;&amp; locatorJson[cursor].isWhitespace()) cursor++ if (cursor &gt;= locatorJson.length &#124;&#124; locatorJson[cursor] != ':') return null cursor++ while (cursor &lt; locatorJson.length &amp;&amp; locatorJson[cursor].isWhitespace()) cursor++ if (cursor &gt;= locatorJson.length &#124;&#124; locatorJson[cursor] != '"') return null cursor++</code><br>`android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/home/HomeFacts.kt:135–141` <code>continue } if (character == '"') break buffer.append(character) cursor++ } return buffer.toString().trim().ifEmpty { null }</code> | The listed invalid inputs all fail/produce null. This hand scanner is not a general JSON validator; malformed text that happens to contain a title token is outside the test.<br>Android test: HomeFactsTest.locatorTitleReadsTheJsonField (partial invalid-input coverage) |

| `publicationProgressWeighsFrontMatterLikeReadium`<br>Whole-book fallback percent weights front matter by Readium-style resource length. | `KudosTests/WorkReadingPositionTests.swift:37` <code>#expect(progress(0, 0) == 0)</code><br>`KudosTests/WorkReadingPositionTests.swift:40` <code>#expect(progress(2, 0) == 3.0 / 23)</code><br>`KudosTests/WorkReadingPositionTests.swift:41` <code>#expect(WorkReadingPosition.cardProgressLabel(progress: progress(2, 0)) == "13%")</code><br>`KudosTests/WorkReadingPositionTests.swift:42` <code>#expect(progress(2, 0.5) == 8.0 / 23)</code><br>`KudosTests/WorkReadingPositionTests.swift:44` <code>#expect(progress(3, 0.9) == 22.0 / 23)</code><br>`KudosTests/WorkReadingPositionTests.swift:45` <code>#expect(WorkReadingPosition.cardProgressLabel(progress: progress(3, 0.9)) == "96%")</code><br>`KudosTests/WorkReadingPositionTests.swift:46` <code>#expect(progress(3, 1) == 1)</code> | **not applicable** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/reader/readium/ReadiumProgressAdapter.kt:21–42` <code>/** Frozen resource identity; only progression changes, never the chapter. */ fun chapterSeekTarget(origin: Locator, progression: Double): Locator = origin.copy( locations = origin.locations.copy(progression = progression.coerceIn(0.0, 1.0), position = null) ) /** Capture a save point. Always populates the cross-platform fallback fields. */ fun toReaderProgress(publication: Publication, locator: Locator): ReaderProgress { val spineIndex = runCatching { publication.readingOrder.indexOfFirst { link -&gt; link.url().toString() == locator.href.toString() } }.getOrDefault(-1).coerceAtLeast(0) val fraction = fallbackScrollFraction(locator) val envelope = ReaderLocatorCodec.encodeEnvelope(locator.toJSON().toString()) val total = locator.locations.totalProgression?.coerceIn(0.0, 1.0) return ReaderProgress( spineIndex = spineIndex, scrollFraction = fraction, locatorJson = envelope, totalProgression = total</code><br>`android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/reader/ReaderProgressMapper.kt:13–16` <code>* Open position matches iOS Readium (`ReadiumReaderView.openBook`): a compatible * locator, otherwise the start of `lastSpineIndex` when it is past the first * spine item. `legacyReaderProgress` is display-only, and the intra-chapter * scroll fraction is not recovered (`ReadiumBook.open`).</code> | These tests reconstruct Readium’s positions for the macOS WKWebView reader. Android has no legacy WKWebView/resourceLengths calculator; it reads totalProgression from its native Readium navigator. No claim of identical resource-length mathematics inside that dependency is made.<br>Android test: no specific matching test located. |

## ReadiumLocatorPreservationTests

| Test — reader guarantee | iOS assertion quote | Verdict | Kotlin implementation quote | Consequence / Android test |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `theArchivedRecordCarriesTheStoredLocator`<br>Export preserves the exact stored locator string on every platform. | `KudosTests/ReadiumLocatorPreservationTests.swift:36` <code>#expect(archived.readiumLocator == Self.locator)</code> | **kept** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/backup/BackupMappers.kt:162` <code>readiumLocator = readiumLocator,</code> | Android test: BackupCompatibilityTest.preservesReadiumLocatorWithoutTreatingItAsFallbackSource |
| `aSnapshotCarryingNoLocatorLeavesTheLocalPositionAlone`<br>Winning newer progress with an absent locator does not erase the local exact position. | `KudosTests/ReadiumLocatorPreservationTests.swift:59` <code>#expect(work.lastSpineIndex == 7)</code><br>`KudosTests/ReadiumLocatorPreservationTests.swift:61` <code>#expect(work.readiumLocator == Self.locator)</code> | **kept** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/backup/BackupMappers.kt:272` <code>readiumLocator = readiumLocator,</code><br>`android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/backup/BackupMergeService.kt:957–961` <code>return base.copy( lastSpineIndex = restored.lastSpineIndex, lastScrollFraction = restored.lastScrollFraction, lastReadDate = restored.lastReadDate, readiumLocator = restored.readiumLocator ?: existing.readiumLocator,</code> | Kotlin’s null-coalescing distinguishes absent/null from the explicit empty string; legacy fields still update.<br>Android test: no specific matching test located. |
| `anExplicitlyEmptyLocatorStillClearsThePosition`<br>An explicit empty locator in winning progress clears the prior position. | `KudosTests/ReadiumLocatorPreservationTests.swift:82` <code>#expect(work.readiumLocator.isEmpty)</code> | **kept** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/backup/BackupMappers.kt:272` <code>readiumLocator = readiumLocator,</code><br>`android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/backup/BackupMergeService.kt:957–961` <code>return base.copy( lastSpineIndex = restored.lastSpineIndex, lastScrollFraction = restored.lastScrollFraction, lastReadDate = restored.lastReadDate, readiumLocator = restored.readiumLocator ?: existing.readiumLocator,</code> | An empty Kotlin String is non-null, so ?: keeps that explicit clear. The test’s newer date/spine passes the progress-presence gate.<br>Android test: no specific matching test located. |
| `aLocatorOnItsOwnCountsAsProgress`<br>A locator alone is enough evidence to apply progress. | `KudosTests/ReadiumLocatorPreservationTests.swift:101` <code>#expect(work.readiumLocator == Self.locator)</code> | **kept** | `android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/backup/BackupMergeService.kt:914–918` <code>val incomingHasProgress = restored.lastReadDate != null &#124;&#124; restored.lastSpineIndex &gt; 0 &#124;&#124; restored.lastScrollFraction &gt; 0.0 &#124;&#124; !restored.readiumLocator.isNullOrBlank() if (!incomingHasProgress) {</code><br>`android/app/src/main/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/backup/BackupMergeService.kt:957–961` <code>return base.copy( lastSpineIndex = restored.lastSpineIndex, lastScrollFraction = restored.lastScrollFraction, lastReadDate = restored.lastReadDate, readiumLocator = restored.readiumLocator ?: existing.readiumLocator,</code> | Android test: BackupCompatibilityTest.preservesReadiumLocatorWithoutTreatingItAsFallbackSource |

| Test — reader guarantee | iOS assertion quote | Verdict | Kotlin implementation quote | Consequence / Android test |

## What is right, and the comparison boundary

Android is right on the core local preservation cases tested here: the same-ID and same-passage note merges preserve displaced text; exact-locator annotation matching does not move a different highlight; absent locator transport does not clear a saved place; an explicit empty locator can clear it. The reading log checkpoints on pause, resumes into one row, excludes background gaps, keys by work UUID, and counts finish transitions rather than reopenings. All five ReadingStatistics tests, all twelve ReadingInsights tests, and all ten ReadingAffinities tests have implemented equivalents. The tables quote those paths, not just similarly named tests.

The checked privacy paths are also right: Library history/favorites drop Hide-mode works, collection pages drop them while retaining Obscure rows, add-work name lists withhold both hidden and obscured identities until reveal, queue search excludes hidden members’ titles/authors, and queue-card faces skip hidden works and redact obscured ones. Collection-picker eligibility has a separate over-exclusion bug; it does not leak hidden titles.

Two absence classifications need care. Fourteen queue tests exercise SwiftUI IndexSet offsets or card-hover drag composition; Android exposes adjacent reorder buttons instead. Four publication-progress tests reconstruct positions for the macOS legacy reader; Android has its own Readium navigator and no such calculator. Two tests concern separate helper features Android does not expose: session-only raw-tag tallies and independent ReadingFavorite target-star CRUD. The latter is not a shipped iOS toggle either: `kudos-ao3-reader/Features/Library/FavoriteAffinityRow.swift:109–112` says <code>// being on this page *is* the favourite. The explicit // `ReadingFavorite` star is a separate axis nothing writes yet // (`ReadingLogService.setFavorite` still has no callers), so this // is not a toggle and does not pretend to be one.</code> Android’s implemented work-star flag and reading-derived affinity rows should not be reported missing because that dormant record API lacks a live writer.

The source-less EPUB finding uses the iOS test’s actual fixture, not an assumed AO3 EPUB. `KudosTests/EPUBTests.swift:13–16` identifies it with <code>static var sampleEPUB: URL { get throws { try #require(Bundle(for: BundleAnchor.self).url(forResource: "sample", withExtension: "epub")) }</code>. I read the ZIP in memory: `KudosTests/Fixtures/sample.epub!OEBPS/content.opf` contains title/author/description/language/subjects/series, with no source or identifier; `ch1.xhtml` and `ch2.xhtml` contain only their headings and Hello/Goodbye paragraphs, with no AO3 link. Android’s own duplicate-import test instead supplies an AO3 source: `android/app/src/test/java/io/github/cidy02/kudos/works/WorkLifecycleTest.kt:1119` <code>sourceUrl = "https://archiveofourown.org/works/424242",</code> and `:1133` <code>val result = importer.importLocalEpub("different-name.epub", syntheticAo3Epub())</code>. That passing-path coverage cannot establish deduplication of the source-less iOS fixture.

The collection-removal sweep itself is implemented correctly, including archive-affirmed re-adds. Its remaining failure is the live clock pipeline: add/remove touch only lastModifiedAt, rename touches that same clock, and the sweep falls back to it while lastMembershipChangedAt is null. The Android unit test manually supplies that separate clock, so it misses the production stamping failure. Likewise, the scalar canonicalizer correctly handles /downloads/id; the acquisition matcher does not apply it to stored rows. These distinctions explain why some nearby rows are kept while the connected lifecycle row is missing.

No time cutoff: all requested files and tests were read. The single **unsure** row is `ReadingQueueTests.discardUnattachedMetadataRemovesOrphansButKeepsRealItems`: Android’s equivalent transient-stub cleanup lifecycle was not established. The 20 not-applicable rows do not certify dependency internals or unimplemented helper APIs.

No builds, test execution, branch switches, commits, pushes, sign-in, AO3 contact, source edits, or TASKS.md edits were performed. These are static control-flow predictions, not live reproductions or visual certification. Only `docs/android-port/audits/A56-result.md` was written.

## The missing ones worth fixing first

1. **Recheck deletion state before every irreversible delete:** a stale Recently Deleted confirmation or an expired-record snapshot can hard-delete a now-restored live work, taking its EPUB, place, and marks with it (`PreservedWorkTests.deletePermanentlyLeavesALiveWorkCollectionAndQueueAlone`).
2. **Put all deletion sweeps behind the persistence gate:** launch currently sweeps outside restore/sync exclusion, so records/files can disappear underneath an active persistence operation (`sweepExpiredIsANoOpWhileTheOperationGateIsHeld`).
3. **Revive matched local works before queueing or resolving them:** the existing-local Work Details branch bypasses restoration, so a failed re-fetch can leave a supposedly reacquired work hidden and approaching its old permanent-deletion deadline (`addToSavedForLaterRevivesRecentlyDeletedWork`, `resolveLocalWorkRevivesASoftDeletedWorkWithoutMaskingAMissingEPUB`).
4. **Recognize the same source-less EPUB on re-import:** the actual iOS fixture currently gets a new UUID while the deleted original and its reading data remain on the purge schedule (`userEPUBImportRevivesRecentlyDeletedDuplicate`).
5. **Match canonical identities on both sides of acquisition:** a stored /downloads/id and incoming /works/id currently split one work’s queues, reading place, and marks across duplicate records (the two canonical-URL acquisition rows).
6. **Finish queue-deletion and normalization bookkeeping:** tombstone removed memberships, clear stale queued flags after the last queue is purged, and reconcile preservation status on add/remove; otherwise works can disappear from ordinary shelves and preservation counts can misclassify copies (`sweepExpiredHardDeletingAQueueTombstonesMembershipsAndUpdatesWork`, `normalizeClearsStaleQueueFlagWithoutMembership`, and the two preservation-status rows).
7. **Provide the non-destructive queue-removal operation:** Android’s sole removeWork path soft-deletes an unsaved/unfavorited last-queue work, where the tested default keeps it live; the EPUB is recoverable for 90 days, but can then be purged (`removeLastQueueMembershipDoesNotDeleteWorkByDefault`).
8. **Stamp and transport the collection membership clock independently of renames:** a cosmetic edit must not suppress a real removal made on another device (`renamingACollectionDoesNotOverrideARemovalElsewhere`).
9. **Warn when deleting a last AO3-unavailable copy:** generic confirmation omits the tested no-longer-available warning (`deleteConfirmationMessageEscalatesWhenAO3Unavailable`).
10. **Let collection pickers offer unkept reading/history works:** observeSavedWorks filters them out before the picker, despite the iOS eligibility rule (both collection candidate tests).
11. **Consolidate colliding tag identities consistently:** the current collision refusal/queue-scoped merge does not perform the tested global link consolidation (`collidingTagRenameCollapsesTheTwoTags`).
12. **Record the newest work on a fandom visit:** date/name updates are right, but the seen-work ID/title are never populated by the live visit writer (`markVisitedUpdatesExistingWatermark`).
13. **Stamp the work as well as the collection when adding membership:** the work’s edit clock is left stale (`addStampsBothSidesModified`).
14. **Restore the tested section display defaults:** History/Favorites are correctly ledger, but other work sections also render ledger unconditionally (`historyAndFavoritesDefaultToLedgerRows`).

## Triage (Claude, 2026-10-15 16:10)

Most of the 24 files' rules are kept on Android. Checked against the code before acting:

- **#1, real (data loss):** fixed in **3fh**. The three hard deletes never rechecked that the
  record was still deleted, so a Delete Forever confirmed after a restore (or an expiry
  snapshot taken before one) erased the live work with its EPUB, place and marks. They now take
  `onlyIfStillDeleted`; Recently Deleted, the expiry sweeps and `hardDeleteAllPending` pass
  `true` (a guard inside `hardDelete` itself broke three tests that delete live records on
  purpose). Test `aStaleDeleteForeverLeavesAWorkRestoredMeanwhileAlone`, failing without it.
- **#2, real:** fixed in **3fh**. The launch sweeps ran outside the persistence gate; they now
  run under it, after any restore or sync holding it.
- **#3 to #14:** brief **3fi** (Codex), each to be confirmed first; #8 may need a stored clock,
  which stops for the owner.
